1 
2317 鴻海精密工業(FOXCONN)

報告日期： 2026/09/03         研究員：曾智楷 (Kevin Tseng) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一、 2Q26 營收、獲利續創同期新高， EPS 4.27 元 
鴻海 2Q26 營收 2.53 兆， QoQ +19% ，YoY +41% ，創歷年同期
新高。就各產別來看， 2Q26 雲端網路產品佔整體營收的 51% ；
消費智能產品佔 29% ；電腦終端產品佔 15% ；元件及其他產品佔
5%，由於第二季為 ICT 產品的傳統淡季， AI 伺服器營收持續走
高，以至於雲端網路產品比重首次突破五成。而獲利方面， 2Q26
營業毛利 1545.3 億， QoQ +18% ，YoY +36% ，毛利率 6.12% ，
QoQ -0.06ppts ，YoY -0.21ppts ，營業利益 948 億， QoQ +25% ，
YoY+68% ，營利率 3.75% ，QoQ +0.18ppts ，YoY +0.6ppts ，創
歷年單季最高。 2Q26 業外淨利 0.63 億，稅後淨利 599.7 億，
QoQ +20% ，YoY +35% ，單季稅後 EPS 4.27 元，創同期新高。

二、進入下半年傳統旺季、 AI 動能續強， 3Q26 營運有
望持續成長  
公司公告 7 月營收 9465 億， MoM +15.2% ，YoY +54.2% ，今年
累計營收 5.59 兆，年增 37.9% 。展望 3Q26 ，公司表示，受惠於
AI 動能持續成長，再加上 ICT 產品進入下半年的傳統旺季，預期
第三季營收將顯著成長。而就各產品別來看，雲端網路產品方面，
受惠於 AI 需求維持高速成長，且 CSP 客戶持續上調資本支出，
帶動 AI 機櫃持續放量，公司預估季對季與年對年都將呈現強勁成
長，並預估第三季 AI 機櫃出貨量將較第二季呈現高雙位數成長。
消費智能產品部分，則由於進入傳統旺季，預期季對季與年對年
皆將有顯著成長。而電腦終端產品方面，由於新產品需求高峰已
過，再加上記憶體供應緊張，預期季對季將呈現略微衰退。預估
公司 3Q26 營收 2.83 兆， QoQ +12.8% ，YoY +37.7% ，毛利率
6.1% ，營利率 3.6% ，稅後淨利 683 億元，單季稅後 EPS 4.92
元。

三、預估 2026 年 EPS 18.63 元 
展望 2026 年，由於 AI 伺服器需求維持強勁需求，訂單能見度提
升，公司維持全年強勁成長的看法不變。 AI 機櫃部分，公司維持
全年 AI 機櫃出貨量倍數以上成長預期，並預計 GB 系列產品需求
將至少延續至明年，整體出貨逐季成長的預期不變。而針對新一
代 AI 機櫃，公司預計第三季開始進行量產準備，第四季開始出貨，
預期在新一代平台的市佔率將進一步提升。 LPU 及 Vera 相關產
投資評等：買進 
目標價格：335 元

品目前處於前期量產準備階段，預計將在第三季開始生產，第四
季逐步放量。而網通產品部分， 800G 以上交換機全年營收有望
倍增， CPO 全光交換機也將按計畫在第三季量產出貨。另外，
ASIC 部分，受惠於新客戶的導入以及增加新的 ASIC 專案，預計
今年 ASIC 專案將有顯著成長，且今年上半年 ASIC 營收佔比已略
高於去年全年。而針對今年營利率展望，公司表示，今年上半年
營業利潤率已達 3.67% ，較去年同期增加 0.67 個百分點，公司預
估今年全年將超越去年 3.2% 的表現。至於資本支出方面，為因應
AI 伺服器機櫃及區域製造需求，預期全年資本支出仍將年增 3 成
以上，用於擴充伺服器、機櫃等關鍵產能，並強化全球製造與自
動化佈局。預估 2026 全年營收 10.8 兆， YoY +33.5% ，毛利率
6.1% ，營利率 3.6% ，稅後淨利 2588 億，全年稅後 EPS 18.63
元。預估 2027 全年營收 12.4 兆， YoY +15% ，毛利率 5.9% ，營
利率 3.3% ，稅後淨利 3116 億，全年稅後 EPS 22.43 元。

四、投資建議為「買進」，目標價 335 元 
考量到 CSP 持續上修 2026 年資本支出展望， AI 伺服器維持強勁
需求，公司預期今年 AI 機櫃出貨量將達成倍數成長，且隨著新一
代 AI 機櫃推出，有望進一步拓展公司在 AI 伺服器領域市佔率。
目前公司股價交易於 2027 年 EPS 約 11~12 倍本益比，評價仍有
提升空間，給予投資建議為買進，目標價 335 元(15x 2027 EPS) 。

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
鴻海精密工業 (FOXCONN) 為全球最大 eCMMS( 電子化 -零元件、
模組機光電垂直整合服務 )廠，在電子代工服務領域 (EMS) 排名全
球第一，市占率超過四成。鴻海近年積極投入「電動車、數位健
康、機器人」三大新興產業以及「人工智慧、半導體、新世代通
訊技術」三項新技術領域，以「 3+3 」結合作為集團重要的長期
發展策略。在 EMS 板塊，鴻海主要競爭對手為和碩聯合科技、
廣達電腦、緯創資通、仁寶電腦、英業達及立訊精密等。

二. 營運概況及展望

表一、鴻海主要產品項目

圖一、鴻海產品營收佔比

資料來源：鴻海、宏遠投顧整理

1. 四大 CSP 上修今年資本支出達 7,450 億美元  
在生成式 AI 商機快速擴張帶動下，全球雲端基礎設施投資進入新
一輪高峰。亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和微軟認為其在先前的鉅額投資已
帶動營收明顯成長，同時預估未來 AI 潛在商機仍持續擴大。其中，
亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和 Meta 已陸續上調 2026 全年投資金額，且
四大雲端服務供應商 (CSP) 總資本支出將上升至 7,450 億美元新
高規模。亞馬遜、微軟、 Alphabet 與 Meta 2026 年第 1、2 季分
別為 1,306 億、 1,701 億美元，同時亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和 Meta 再
上修 2026 全年資本支出金額， 2026 年資本支出預估將達 7,450
億美元。而根據 TrendForce 最新產業研究，全球主要雲端服務
供應商 (CSP) 為加速建置 AI 基礎設施，資本支出預估於 2026 年
將年增 98% ，2027 年再成長 50% 至 1.3 兆美元左右，年增率收
斂的主要原因，除了反映高基期因素之外，部分可歸咎為鉅額資

本支出對四大 CSP 財務衝擊日益擴大。然而，在 AI Inference 、
Agentic AI 、自研 ASIC 和新一代 AI 模型持續推升算力需求的情
況下，預期整體 CSP 投資金額將續創新高紀錄，產業成長有望從
原先以 GPU 擴張為主，轉為包含 AI server 、液冷散熱、先進封
裝、高速互聯、電源和記憶體等基礎設施全面升級的態勢。

圖二、 2024-2027 年全球九大 CSP 資本支出總額預估 (單位 :十億美元 )

資料來源： TrendForce 、宏遠投顧整理

2. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31% 
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產業研究，近期超大型 CSP 、
Tier-2 資料中心業者對 NVIDIA GB/VR 等整櫃式 AI server 方案的
採購意願明顯提高，且 Google 、AWS 新一代 ASIC 平台於 2026
下半年陸續放量，加上中系業者擴大採用本地 AI 方案以滿足雲端
大型語言模型 (LLM) AI 需求，促使 TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出貨年成長幅度，從原先的 28% 更新為近 31% 。北美五大
業者 2026 年的資本支出多翻倍提升，反映出大型 CSP 廠持續擴
建 AI 資料中心、 GPU 叢集、液冷散熱等新一代基礎設施，以應
對生成式 AI 及大型模型快速成長帶來的算力需求。其中，
Google 2026 至 2027 年都將側重自家 TPU 晶片發展， 2027 年出
貨量有望繼續高速成長。 AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為
GPU AI server 主力，自研 ASIC 也穩定出貨，預期至 2027 年量
能將再擴大。 Meta 2026 年主要採用 NVIDIA GB/VR 、 AMD 
Helios 整櫃式方案，自研 ASIC 布局預計至 2027 年將明顯加速，
為該公司長期降低 AI 基礎設施成本、提升推論效率的重要策略。

### Page 1

Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co.

SEMICON Taiwan: proliferating AI boosting 
the manufacturing innovation

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 3,100 TWD | Price: 2,420 TWD

HPC computing drives holistic manufacturing innovation 
SEMICON Taiwan kicked off on August 31st with IC Forum and Silicon Photonics Summit, 
both featuring TSMC’s presentations. At the session in IC Forum, TSMC shared its view 
that the data center power capacity will accelerate from 5-6GW per year from ‘15-24 to 
30-40GW per year from ‘25-30, reaching 300GW installed base by ’30 as AI training 
scales toward multimodal (5x per annum LLM compute growth). In addition, it sees 
robust AI inference demand, with token consumption growing ~500x since ’22 led by 
agents and reasoning and further compounding by 24x to 120 quadrillion tokens by ‘30. 
TSMC has AI platform across logic (A13, A12, N2U). Si-technology for HBM scaling (from 
N12 for HBM4 to N3 for HBM5) and system integration (e.g. SoIC with 11 tape outs for 
GPU/CPU by 4Q26 and >14x reticle size CoWoS by ‘29) and compute fabric (e.g. CPO). 
Silicon photonics acceleration to enable faster I/O scaling 
TSMC believes the optical I/O is becoming prerequisite for AI/HPC system as the I/O gap 
is widening between the compute scaling (3x every 2 years) and I/O scaling (1.4x every 
two years). Its COUPE platform and a broad photonics device/PDK library facilitate its 
customer designs to enhance the connectivity through two complementary paths – 
“faster and narrowing” with higher per lane speed (200Gbps to 400Gbps) and lane count 
(128+ lanes) and “slower and wider” by increasing wavelengths from 1 to 16+ through 
WDM. To allow further production ramp and wider adoption of the technology platform, 
TSMC has lowered COUPE grating coupler insertion loss while improved edge coupler 
performance and sees wafer level testing is critical to reduce the wasted packaging cost. 
Stock at mid-cycle supported by robust AI demand pipeline 
We maintain our Buy rating with PO at NT$3,100 on 20x '27 P/E. The share valuation is 
undemanding at 16x '27 P/E on strong technology leadership and extending AI visibility.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
TSMC was founded in 1987 and is listed in Taiwan 
(2330TT) and the US (TSM/NYS).TSMC is the largest and 
global leader in integrated circuit (IC) manufacturing. As a 
build-to-order foundry, it provides a wide range of value-
add activities: IC manufacturing, mask-making, IC design 
services, turnkey solutions, and process development. We 
attribute its success to its proven, winning business model, 
unparalleled scale advantage, optimized execution, and 
technology scope and depth. 5 shares = 1 ADR.

Investment Rationale 
The ongoing semi content growth in mobile, rise of artificial 
intelligence (AI), and proliferation of Internet of Things (IoT) 
should result in sustainable upside in aggregate computing 
power globally. TSMC, as the leading contract manufacturer 
of semiconductor chips, is in a good position to capitalize. 
We are structurally positive on TSMC and expect it to 
sustain over 15% growth with rising FCF from 2026 
onwards.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co. (TSMWF / TSM) 
Our PO for TSMC is NT$3,100 per share (US$590 per ADR), based on 20x 2027E P/E. 
TSMC's valuation has traded in the range of 10-26x PE from 2017. 20x is in the mid to 
high end of the historical range, above the average P/E of 18x. We think a PE above the 
average is justified given TSMC's enhanced industry position and mid-40% 5-year CAGR 
in AI should underpin a rerating

Downside risks to our price objective are: (1) greater-than-expected slowdown in global 
smartphone/consumer electronics demand, (2) Intel's potential insourcing strategy and 
ambitions in foundry service, (3) execution risks on advanced nodes, and (4) higher than 
expected tariffs.

## Document 061: 260831_daiwa_ Kinsus

Source PDF: all report pdf/260831_daiwa_ Kinsus.pdf

### Page 1

Summary: We hosted an online investor conference call for Kinsus after trading hours on 
31 August. We believe that management’s current revenue target is 15% QoQ growth in 
3Q26 (vs. market expectation of 10% QoQ). Regarding the investigation into Unimicron’s 
original place of production, the company noted that it has not seen any order shift from 
end customers. However, it believes this event will not be a negative factor for other 
substrate makers. In addition, Kinsus’ 9% share price decline today has nothing to do with 
the investigation into Unimicron. Rather, it is more likely related to its parent company’s 
plan to sell 16 million shares in Kinsus (announced last Friday). Kinsus believes this was 
driven by financial management considerations, and not by any specific intention to reduce 
its stake in Kinsus. If Kinsus’ share price continues to rise, management does not rule out 
the possibility that its parent company may trim its stake again. Overall, we believe Kinsus’ 
fundamentals remain largely in line with our expectations, with raw material prices 
continuing to rise and substrate supply tightening gradually. We maintain our Buy (1) rating 
on the stock, with a 12-month target price of TWD1,068, based on a 36x PER on the 1-
year forward EPS. Key downside risks include weaker-than-expected demand for memory, 
PCs, gaming GPUs, and AI servers.

We hosted an online conference call for Kinsus after trading hours on 31 August. Overall, 
we believe the business outlook to be largely on track with our previous note (link here, 
published on 31 July). Below are our key takeaways from the conference.

Recent updates:  
• We believe the company has revised up its 3Q26 revenue growth target from 10% QoQ 
to 15% QoQ, reflecting a better-than-expected benefit from price increases. Looking 
ahead to 4Q26, management still expects growth of 10% QoQ, in line with its previous 
comments. 
• Revenue mix in 2Q26: ABF 45%, BT 38%, and contact lens 17%. Looking ahead, the 
company expects the revenue mix from ABF to gradually increase to 50–55% in 2027.

Price hike & margin expansion: 
• The company maintains its target of a 7-8% ASP increase per quarter in 2H26 to reflect 
rising costs of raw materials, including glass fibre, copper foil, drill bits, and ABF build-up 
film. 
• Although some competitors have highlighted a much larger magnitude of price hikes, 
Kinsus has not seen a similar level of increase. The company also believes the price 
hike level for ABF will be higher than that for BT in 2H26, whereas in 1H26 the price hike 
for BT was higher than that for ABF. 
• Looking into 2H26 and 2027, the company expects raw material ASPs to continue rising. 
As such, single-digit price hikes should be possible, with raw materials currently 
accounting for 30% of BT’s COGS. In addition, the driver of ASP increases is expected 
to gradually shift from raw material inflation to substrate capacity shortages.  
• In terms of gross margin expansion, Kinsus believes this will be driven mainly by an 
improved product mix and rising utilisation rates, rather than price hikes. This is similar 
to Unimicron. However, NYPCB’s gross margin expansion could be driven by internal 
price hikes within the Nanya Group.

Country

31 August 2026

MEMO 
 Key takeaways from investor conference call

### Page 2

Kinsus Interconnect Technology (3189 TT) 
31 August 2026 
2

Utilisation rates (UTR): 
• ABF: The company expects the UTR to increase from 85% (beginning of 2026) to 95% 
(at the end of 2026). 
• BT: The company expects the UTR to increase from 75% (beginning of 2026) to 85% (at 
the end of 2026).

Capacity expansion plan 
• Given capacity constraints, Kinsus will gradually expand its ABF capacity. It currently 
has around a 40 million ABF substrate capacity per month and plans to increase this to 
80 million by 2029. 
• According to management, the major equipment pull-in cycle will occur in 2027. After 
that, most equipment will be subsidised by end customers, and the depreciation 
expense should decline to TWD6.5-7.0bn from 2028 onward, versus TWD7.1-7.2bn in 
2026 and 2027. 
• As depreciation currently accounts for 15% of COGS, Kinsus believes it will reduce its 
selling prices slightly after clients begin subsidising the equipment, as the benefit will be 
shared between clients and Kinsus, at 7-8% each.

New growth opportunities – ABF 
• We believe Kinsus will secure a higher allocation for both CPU and GPU products from 
a global leading GPU brand in its next-generation products, given the current industry’s 
supply constraints. 
• The company also believes it will provide additional support to other GPU brands for 
their GPU products from 2027. 
• Outside of GPUs, the company sees less progress in the ASIC segment for now 
compared with GPUs. It has secured around a 10% allocation for one major ASIC 
product, but progress in other ASIC products remains relatively limited.

New growth opportunities – BT  
• The company believes pluggable optical modules and market share gains (as 
competitors gradually exit the BT market) will be key growth drivers for Kinsus. 
• According to management, the BT substrate used in optical modules will have 14 layers, 
compared with the mainstream 4-layer format today. Its existing equipment can support 
both 4-layer and 14-layer BT substrates. However, the company may need to add some 
routing capacity to support optical module demand.

We see positive implications for Bizlink from Credo's comments
on expanding AEC   and emerging ALC opportunities.
FY1Q27 (ended July 2026) results:
• Credo (CRDO.US, not covered) reported revenue of US$479mn (+10% QoQ/
+115% YoY), roughly in line with the consensus of US$474mn and guidance
of US$465-475mn.
• Non-GAAP GM was 68% (-0.3ppt QoQ) in line with consensus at 68% and

guidance at 67-69%.
• Non-GAAP NP was US$236mn (+4% QoQ/+140% YoY), roughly in line with
consensus at US$232mn.
• Four customers each contributed more than 10% of Credo's revenue in
FY1Q27, i.e., 33%, 28%, 13% and 10%, respectively.
FY2Q27 and FY27 outlook:
•  FY2Q27 revenue is guided to be US$525-535mn (+11% QoQ/+98% YoY at the
midpoint, largely in line with consensus of US$526mn), non-GAAP GM of 67-
69% (vs. consensus at 67.5%) and opex of US$100-105mn.

• For FY27, Credo now expects total revenue to grow >85% YoY (vs.
consensus at ~86% YoY), with an  inflection in 2H mainly from optical
revenue (>US$600mn for FY27 from Zero-Flap Optics, silicon photonics PICs
and optical DSPs, with each to contribute >US$100mn).
• Credo expects its FY27 GM to stay flat YoY, while operating expenses might
increase by ~55% YoY.
• 1.6T AEC might start to emerge from 2HFY27.
• ALCs and OmniConnect gearboxes remain on track for initial revenue from

FY28, management believes both are multi-billion dollar opportunities for
Credo.
• It also sees increasing customer activity on NPO for scale up network, with
confirmed PIC design wins for ramp in FY28.
Bizlink Read-across:

upgrade and its penetration into more hyperscalers and neoclouds, as well as
the reiterated timing of ALC ramp in FY28.

CHPT
Valuation
We value CHPT using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum and the structural shift towards high-end
testing. Our target price of NT$4,350 is derived from applying 35x to 2027E EPS, implying the company's average forward PE
since 2024 +1 SD, which we believe is justified given the company's entrenched position in the 2nm/3nm testing segment,
rising contribution from US AI GPU & AI ASIC projects, and the unique competitive advantage of its "All-in-House" vertical

integration model. With these structural drivers in place, and as we believe the market is starting to value it as an AI/HPC play
(rather than a consumer electronics stock), we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its historical averages.
Risks
Our quant system rates the CHPT stock as High Risk for its share price volatility.
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price, and to our earnings forecasts, include: 1) margin

compression from lower-than-expected utilization rates; 2) execution delays in concentrated US AI GPU and ASIC programs;
3) intensifying competition in testing boards from global peers; and 4) structural gross margin dilution from a shift towards
high-end burn-in boards.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 074: 260903_daiwa_ Laiyi

Source PDF: all report pdf/260903_daiwa_ Laiyi.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-03
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Consumer Discretionary

What’s new: We received an update from Lai Yih’s management after its 
upbeat 2Q26 results (see memo, 25 August). We are more confident in its 
revenue and operating profit returning to growth YoY in 2H26E and 2027E 
on new model launches from its key clients, a new client win and better 
seasonality approaching in 4Q26E, and thus, upgrade Lai Yih to Buy (1) 
from Hold (3).

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results key highlight: better-than-expected 
client and product mix. In 2Q26, we noticed that Lai Yih’s revenue and 
gross margin beat expectations on more favourable client and product mix. 
Its operating profit of TWD1.203bn (+566.6% QoQ and +8.7% YoY) beat 
our and Bloomberg consensus estimates by a respective 26.6% and 
41.1%. Lai Yih’s client mix for 2Q26 included Adidas (53.4%; -2.5% YoY), 
Hoka (36%; +9.0% YoY, record-high revenue mix level), Converse (7.8%; -
41.5% YoY) and New Balance (2.8%; -19.6% YoY).

Brighter 2H26E and 2027E outlook with rising new model launches 
from existing clients; we assume one new client in 4Q26. We believe 
3Q26E will be off-peak season for Lai Yih, but expect its revenue 
momentum to pick up QoQ in 4Q26E, along with gross margin expansion 
QoQ. Given the company’s track record with Adidas and Hoka, 
management noted it is in discussion with several potential new clients; we 
assume one new client to onboard in 4Q26. Lai Yih aims for 2027 
shipments to return to 2025 levels. Despite continuing inflation, the 
company targets to gain more high-end shoes with higher ASP to drive its 
overall ASP and gross margin expansion. Besides, Hoka will start to initiate 
new products in 2H26, which we believe will be positive to Lai Yih’s gross 
margin. Our 2026-27E EPS remain almost unchanged, and we introduce 
our 2028 forecasts, calling for EPS growth of 10% YoY.

What we recommend: We upgrade Lai Yih to Buy (1) from Hold (3) and 
raise our 12M TP to TWD230 (from TWD160) mainly on improving 
fundamentals expected from 2H26E. Due to a recovering earnings 
trajectory, we now use a target PER of 18x (from 15x), vs. its past-3-year 
range of 12-33x (average PER of 20x), applied to our 1-year forward EPS. 
We forecast an earnings CAGR of 18.5% over 2026-28 (previously -1.6% 
over 2025-27E). Downside risks: weaker-than-expected end demand; 
lower-than-expected market share gain; inflation.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 15-22% below the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely as we are more conservative on revenue and gross 
margin. However, we believe we are more positive on Lai Yih’s client win 
capability riding on the product mix optimisation trend.

3 September 2026

Upgrading: improving fundamentals

➢ 2Q26 results beat expectations in revenue and gross margin 
➢ Brighter order visibility in 2H26E and 2027E; new client in 2H26 
➢ Upgrading to Buy (1) from Hold (3); raising 12M TP to TWD230

Company profile 
Established in 1987, Lai Yih is a footwear manufacturer that collaborates with Adidas (56.4% of total 
revenue in 2025), Hoka (28.5%), Converse (12.4%) and Uniqlo. Its expertise lies in the production of 
high-quality vulcanised shoes, athletic footwear, cold pressed shoes, and specialised functional 
footwear. Lai Yih Group is headquartered in Taichung (Taiwan) and its production plants and 
development centres for clients are mainly based in Vietnam (91% of total shipment in 2025), Myanmar 
(7%) and Indonesia (1%).

ESG analysis

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
The CEO and chairperson of Lai Yih is not the same individual. We note that the CEO, Mr. Lin,
has 35 years of experience in this industry, which is a positive sign from an executive
perspective.
Capital management 1 Lai Yih's dividend payout ratio was 44-70% over 2022-25, which we see as rational due to its
capacity expansion plans.
Related party &
transaction 1 Revenue generated from related-party transactions was less than 1% of total revenue during
2022-25, and we note no suspicious activity.
G Supply chain
management 2
In supply chain management, the company emphasises product quality and safety. Due to its
focus on supply chain transparency, it has designated sales specialists to provide suppliers with
prompt responses to issues.
E Materials sourcing &
efficiency 2
For raw materials and energy usage, the company prioritises environmental sustainability. Its
production processes adhere to safety certifications and standardized procedures, while it strives
to improve energy efficiency and reduce pollution.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 2

For the month of September 2026, we estimate AirTAC’s monthly revenue will increase 
to Rmb827mn (+6% MoM, +17% YoY)/NT$3,930mn (+6% MoM, +27% YoY) 
We expect September revenue will go up by 6% MoM to Rmb827mn/NT$3,930mn, 
mainly driven by the improvement in daily pneumatic revenue (Rmb30.9mn in Sep vs. 
Rmb 29.1mn in Aug) despite the same number of working days. The improvement in 
daily pneumatic revenue is driven by the strong growth from key customers, particularly 
in electronics (we also expect peers to see strong demand in electronics, see here), 
followed by batteries and machine tools, and we expect demand to improve following 
the inventory pull-in after the Europe summer holiday.

Investment view: Strong YoY momentum in electronics could drive overall growth. 
Maintain positive view on AirTAC 
We reiterate our Buy rating on AirTAC, as we continue to see key end-segments 
performance tracking ahead of the company’s 2026 full year guidance of 20%+ YoY

### Page 3

growth (see here), with electronics/battery/machine tools delivering 20%/64%/31% 
YTD YoY growth while total revenue in Rmb is tracking at 25% YTD YoY growth. The 
strong YTD performance is in line with our expectation that AirTAC is a key beneficiary 
of the factory automation/industrial automation localization trend in mainland China, as 
well as benefiting from the prolonged upcycle supported by government stimulus 
policies. However, we maintain caution on the potential of exaggerated orders (including 
advance or duplicate orders), and will continue to monitor the underlying end-demand 
closely. 
Furthermore, we believe the company is well-positioned to continue to gain market 
share in the China pneumatic market, particular given its pricing strategy, (1) its 
pneumatic products pricing remain competitively priced at 10%+ below international 
peers, and (2) the company has maintained stable pricing in China (China accounts for 
~95% of total revenue in 2026E per GSe) amid the inflationary cost environment, while 
the company will continue to enhance internal operational efficiency to mitigate the 
rising cost risk (see here). As a result, we continue to see backlog accumulating and 
expect the company to maintain a high UTR to digest these orders. 
Maintain Buy on AirTAC with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$1,925. 
Investment Thesis - AirTAC 
AirTAC is the 2nd-largest pneumatic supplier in mainland China with 28% market share 
in 2025, outpacing most local competitors, owing to its low-cost yet high quality 
product, and service quality reputation in most key cities in mainland China. We forecast 
its market share will reach 30% in 2026E and expect AirTAC to be the structural winner 
in a deflationary market, due to its: (1) strong market growth and leadership: AirTAC 
continues to expand its market share in mainland China driven by competitive pricing 
that is more than 10% lower vs. international peers (with 45%+ GM / 30-35% OPM) and 
high demand elasticity among mainland China customers; and (2) superior cost structure 
and financial health: with best-in-class cash cost and a solid balance sheet, featuring 
positive net cash over FCF since 2023/2024, we believe AirTAC is well-equipped to 
weather industry downturns and capitalize on market restructuring. 
Overall, we believe AirTAC will see better revenue/profitability in the long term given its 
share gainer position in the China pneumatic market and potential to gain share in the 
linear guide market (improved demand in the next 1-2 years). This should lead to 
strengthened valuation, vs. its recent trading range (trading below its 10-year avg. P/E 
multiple). We are Buy rated on AirTAC. 
Price target risks and methodology - AirTAC 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,925 is based on a target P/E of 26.0x on 
our 2027E EPS (0.25x STDV above avg of the last pneumatic upcycle). 
Key downside risks: (1) Stronger competition from peers; (2) sharper-than-expected 
Rmb depreciation, and (3) a longer-than-expected end-market demand downtrend in 
mainland China.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

WinWay Technology
Valuation
We value WinWay using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum and likely expansion of its addressable
market. Our target price of NT$11,500 is derived from applying c.41x to 2H27E-1H28E EPS, higher than the upper end of its
long-term PE range, which we believe is justified given the company's entrenched position in the high-end AI GPU testing
supply chain, rising contribution from coaxial socket and SLT solutions, and the increasing value of test content per module.

With these structural drivers in place, and as we believe the market is starting to value it as an AI/HPC play (rather than a
consumer electronics stock), we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its historical averages.
Risks
Our quant rating system rates the WinWay stock High Risk based on its historical volatility.
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price and our earnings forecasts include: 1) client and

sector concentration – specifically any delays in US AI GPU product roadmap could impact the business outlook for WinWay; 2)
intensifying competition in coaxial and hypersockets from global peers; 3) faster-than-expected testing algorithm
optimization, which could shorten testing durations and reduce the anticipated hardware demand multiplier; and 4) delay of
MEMS Phase 2 mass production.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

and (4) an ongoing project with a small-scale ASIC client.
PCB/CCL sector read-across
We retain our positive view on the optical transceivers’ mSAP demand outlook, while our

supply chain checks recently suggested mSAP as a key bottleneck for the 1.6T optical
transceivers supply chain now, whose product pricing has gone up by 30%+ YTD with
good profitability (our checks and Compeq’s comments suggest the GM for 1.6T optical
transceivers mSAP are generally 40-45%+). We believe ZDT has the best opportunity to
gain more market share in this high-growth and profitable market (we expect TAM for
1.6T+ optical transceivers mSAP to go up from US$0.8bn in 2026 to US$2.9bn in 2028,

implying 94% CAGR), considering it has the most aggressive mSAP capacity expansion
plan (we expect ZDT to expand mSAP capacity by 5x in the next two years) and the
company’s best-in-class BT/mSAP production technology, which could become the
company’s key revenue driver (we expect mSAP to account for ~8%/10%+ of ZDT
revenue in 2027/28 from ~2% in 2026) and margin driver (mSAP GM should be 40-45%+
vs. ZDT 1H26 GM at only ~22+%).
We also expect CCL suppliers, such as EMC and TUC, to benefit from the strong optical

transceiver demand, and we expect both companies to gain more share with more CCL
capacity to be ready in 2027/28 (see here and here).
Overall, we retain our positive view on ZDT’s business outlook not only on mSAP
products, but also seeing a significant margin expansion opportunity from the high-end
AI PCB and ABF substrate products, where we also see a strong demand outlook (see our
AI PCB industry report here and our ABF substrate industry report here).

We maintain our Buy ratings on ZDT, EMC and TUC with unchanged 12m TPs of
NT$925/NT$10,200/NT$3,010.

### Page 3

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Zhen Ding Technology 
Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT) is the global leading PCB supplier (top 1 in market share in 
sales) with 7-10% market share, and started its ABF substrate business from 2023 in 
Shenzhen, China; it has the highest ABF revenue exposure to the China market compared 
to all other ABF substrate suppliers. We believe ZDT will hold more than 40% ABF 
substrate market share in China by 2027E (up from 31% in 2024) and benefit the most 
from the strong and growing China ABF substrate market (53% 2025E-27E CAGR) due to 
the increasing in-sourcing ratio for the AI computing ASIC/GPU market in China. We also 
expect the increasing foldable smartphone penetration rate in the long term to drive 
ZDT’s FPC TAM, and account for 50% of the company’s total revenue in 2027E. We see 
further revenue upside potential from foldable phones. We are Buy rated mainly due to 
our positive view on the company’s China ABF substrate business opportunity, which 
should not only drive ZDT’s revenue growth, but also improve its OPM level in the long 
term. We see upside for the stock price considering the company’s solid growth 
opportunity, and our TP implied P/E multiple is still lower than the company’s past 10+ 
year P/E average, which we believe is fair considering the potential upside from China 
ABF substrate market and increasing foldable phone penetration rate.  
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$925 is based on a SOTP valuation methodology, 
including (1) 3.5x blended 2028E P/B for ABF & BT substrate, (2) 20x 2028E P/E for 
high-growth AI server/switch related PCB (in line with industry average), and (3) 8x 
2028E P/E for low-growth consumer electronics PCB (in-line with industry downcycle 
average valuation).  
Key downside risks include: (1) weaker-than-expected iPhone demand or slower 
content upgrade, (2) delay in new substrate capacity or slower-than-expected yield rate 
ramp up progress, and (3) fiercer competition in China ABF market. 
Investment Thesis - Elite Material  
EMC, a key supplier of high-end HDI material (70%+ market share) and SLP material 
(90%+ market share), has been focusing on the high-speed switch/server CCL market for 
3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and 
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+% 
in the Whitley/Eagle Stream platforms. EMC is also proactively expanding its share in the 
switch market (40%/30%+ in 2024/23 vs. <5% before 2019), with an expanding AI 
customer base (only one 400G customer in 3Q20, to being one of the largest suppliers in 
2023), which should continue to benefit the company’s top/bottom line growth over 
time. We believe EMC’s leading position in the AI server CCL market should drive strong 
revenue growth and GM/OPM expansion in the long term, given that it is the major 
supplier for all Nvidia/Google/AWS AI server projects, and we expect it to maintain its 
leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid 
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the 
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a 
solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive. 
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) 
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and 
7 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs Taiwan PCB/CCLFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

(3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Elite Material  
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$10,200 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(in line with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in the past 3 years).  
Key downside risks:  (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) rising 
trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and (3) 
rising competition from mainland China peers. 
Investment Thesis - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL 
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+ 
market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market 
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to 
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of products for 
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong 
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for 
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view. 
Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise 
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the 
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.  
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL 
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch 
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$3,010 is based on a 22x 2027E P/E (+2x 
STDV higher than the past 3-year industry average P/E multiple).  
Key downside risks:  (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL segment; 
(2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch shipments 
globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

M
Update
Advantech  | Asia Pacific
August Sales -8% MoM/+88%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Advantech's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August sales were NT$10,419mn (-8% MoM/+88% YoY),
• QTD revenue reached 77% of MSe, at NT$28,185mn (+8% QoQ/+59% YoY),
and 76% of consensus, at NT$28,371mn (+9% QoQ/+60% YoY).
• By region, N.A (+106%) outperformed, while other markets, including Europe
(+52%), China (+67%), Taiwan (+96%), Japan (+73%), Korea (+79%)  and

emerging markets (+75%),  also performed strongly.
• By segment, Intelligent Systems was +93% YoY,   IoT Automation was +36%
YoY, while  Intelligent Service grew single-digit% YoY. The Embedded Sector
grew 61% YoY, of which Embedded IoT grew 83% YoY, while Applied
Computing grew 37% YoY.
Our view:
• We think its 3Q26 revenue is tracking ahead of market expectations and
more likely to approach 20-25% QoQ growth.
• Management attributed the strength in August to demand from sectors

including semi, AI datacenter, energy infrastructure, and factory automation.
• B/B ratio in August was stronger than that in July, continuing to point to a
strong revenue outlook in the coming quarters.
• We remain constructive on Advantech, as it is well positioned to benefit
from accelerating IoT market growth amid edge AI proliferation.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Advantech (2395.TW)
Base case, 30x 2027e P/E. We view our target multiple as justified by our 30% earnings CAGR
forecast for 2025-28. It implies a PEG of 1.0, vs. the stock's average one-year forward P/E of
25x in 2015-22 and the earnings CAGR of 8% during that period, with a PEG of 3.5.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected macroeconomic conditions
n More top- and bottom-line growth from the platform and applications

Risks to Downside
n Weaker macro environment
n Slower progress in edge AI implementation

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
TFT-LCD Panel Price Outlook
for Sep-26: TVs, Monitors and
NBs Flat MoM
Panel pricing should stat flat for major applications in the
coming months. We believe that the glass opportunities in
advanced packaging are already in the price for panel makers,
though the timing for mass production and intensifying
competition could be potential risk factors.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Derrick Yang

75" 4K 65" 4K 55" FHD 50" FHD 43" FHD 32" HD
Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
TV panel prices to stay flat MoM in September: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and
75" TV panel prices are all expected to stay flat for the month of September. This is
consistent with our expectations. On the demand side, although overall demand is
lackluster, there is still some seasonal restocking demand from brand customers to
prepare for  year-end promotion campaigns. On the supply side, panel makers remain

disciplined on output control and plan some annual maintenance to adjust
utilization during the coming October holidays in China. On a quarterly average
basis, we expect TV panel prices to decline by low-single-digit % QoQ in 3Q26.
IT panel prices to stay flat MoM in  September: Monitor and notebook panel prices
are expected to stay flat MoM during the month. Both applications are  seeing
slower demand in  2H26 post the earlier restocking  in 1H26 amid concerns about

increasing component costs. That said, panel makers are under cost pressure and
reluctant to given more price concessions. On a quarterly average basis, we expect
IT panel prices to remain flat QoQ in 3Q26.
Risk/reward less attractive: We think the current valuation for Taiwanese panel
names AUO (2409.TW; 1.5x P/B) and Innolux (3481.TW; 1.7x P/B) have already baked
in some expectations of glass in advanced packaging starting to make revenue
contribution in 2028. Nonetheless, some supply chain players think this might take

longer, and there seem to be many suppliers from different supply chains vying for
this opportunity as well, which could be a potential risk to monitor. In China, we
prefer BOE (000725.SZ; 1.4x P/B) over TCL (000100.SZ; 1.7x P/B), on its scale
advantage and faster progress in glass core substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Seasonal restocking along with disciplined output control should help stabilize
TV panel prices in the coming months.
IT panel prices are expected to stay roughly flat in coming quarters as well.
Potential opportunities for glass in advanced packaging seem  already  priced in,
with the risk in commercialization timing and competition worth monitoring.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
TV Panel Prices Trending Down in 3Q26
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March. April, May and June were largely flat, while
July was down 2% MoM and August was down 1% MoM, in line with our expectation that
TV panel prices would start trending down from 3Q26.  That said, we do not expect a
significant decline as supply-side discipline should provide downside support.

Below-Seasonal Panel Shipments in 2H26
Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have typically been split 49%:51% between 1H
and 2H. In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H, as TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June-July, and China's 618 shopping festival. We see no clear evidence that these events

will materially boost global TV demand, but they have historically shifted shipment timing
across quarters. As a result, we expect the restocking momentum seen since the start of
the year to fade, with panel order momentum slowing in 2H26.
Display Fab Utilization To Be at ~85% in 3Q26
Stronger TV panel shipments supported display fab utilization of ~80% in 4Q24 and 82%
in 1Q25. Utilization declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands adopted a more cautious stance

following earlier pull-ins and as the impact of China's trade-in program moderated.
Utilization rebounded to 80-85% in 3Q25 as brands restocked for year-end promotions
and remained at similar levels in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as in 1Q26.
Industry utilization stayed broadly flat QoQ, at 80-85% in 2Q26, reflecting continued
supply discipline, while we expect a slight uptick in 3Q26.
Over the medium to long term, we continue to believe the display industry is undergoing

structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted their focus from top-line growth to
profitability, while lower government subsidies have reinforced supply discipline. We
believe investors increasingly recognize that the industry's production-to-order model can
remain sustainable, particularly as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs.
multiyear cycles in the previous decade. Consolidation actions, including LG Display's sale
of its Gen 8.5 Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp's closure of its Gen 10 Sakai fab, also

support a healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural

### Page 5

Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Fair After Recent Correction
We view risk-reward as broadly balanced following the recent correction in panel stocks.
Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We rate AUO Equal-weight with a PT of NT
$27.00, based on a 1.4x 2026e P/B multiple. While we believe the current round of
TV panel price increases is nearing an end, major panel makers should continue to
demonstrate solid supply discipline, providing downside support. We appreciate

AUO's efforts to diversify its business, but meaningful earnings contributions are
likely to take time.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We prefer BOE due to its scale advantage,
higher exposure to display, and faster progress in glass core substrates. Our PT of
Rmb9.30 is based on 2.5x 2026e P/B, which we view as reasonable given our
estimated ROE of 5-8% over 2026-28 compared with BOE's average P/B of 1.1x and
ROE of 2-4% during 2023-25.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): In semiconductors, our supply chain checks

suggest that Innolux is participating in a foundry customer's glass core substrate
project. However, we do not expect meaningful contributions before 2028, and
additional competitors could enter as the industry moves toward mass production.
We maintain our Equal-weight rating and PT of NT$60.00, based on a 2.1x 2026e
P/B multiple.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Ryan Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-

markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 0.7x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B). The stock is trading at 1.7x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
Our Underweight thesis reflects weaker PC, smartphone and automotive demand,

gross margin pressure, and a lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's
OLED DDI roadmap appears more competitive than those of its key Korean peers
in terms of both performance and cost, helping it gain share in Apple's iPhone
supply chain.

Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where We Could Be Wrong
• Stronger end-demand: If end-demand for major applications turns out to be
better than expected, there could be stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

### Page 6

Aspeed Technology (5274.TWO)
09 September 2026 Citi Research
6
Aspeed Technology
Company description
Established in 2004 and listed in 2013, Aspeed is a leading IC design
company based in Taiwan focusing on baseboard management controller
(BMC) solutions. Aspeed started the business by targeting datacenter
servers/white-box markets and has been working closely with server ODMs.
Following the acquisition of Emulex Pilot from AVGO/Broadcom in 2016,

Aspeed successfully expanded its product lines, IP pool as well as customer
base and became the largest BMC supplier with c.70% of global market share.
Other than BMC, Aspeed is also broadening its product portfolio by including
bridge IC (BIC; or mini-BMC), platform firmware resilience (PFR) solutions and
input/output (I/O) expander, aiming to maximize the dollar content it could
generate per server motherboard.
Investment strategy
We rate Aspeed shares a Buy. As a leading BMC supplier with c.70%+ global

market share, it has been riding the strong momentum underpinned by a
recovery of general-purpose servers and upticks in AI servers. We expect the
growth to continue. Apart from being in a dominant position for the supply of
upcoming GB200 server BMCs with higher dollar content and better capex
efficiency, new product launches including AST2700, PFR, I/O expander and
Cupola 360, as well as steady growth in general-purpose servers should also
act as key drivers for the company.

Valuation
We value Aspeed at NT$25,000 based on a historical mid-cycle multiple
(since 2020) of 50x 2028E EPS, as we believe it fairly reflects the company’s
earnings performance and industry dynamics, secular growth trends, and
Aspeed’s strong supply chain position, as well as robust earnings growth
amid margin expansions.
Risks
Our quant rating system rates the Aspeed stock High Risk based on its
volatility over the past year, which we think is unwarranted given the

company's dominance and outlook in the BMC sector. Key downside risks to
our earnings forecasts and target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected
growth in servers; 2) slower-than-expected CPU platform migration; 3) peer
competition; and 4) limited progress in product diversification.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 106: 260909_daiwa_EPM

Source PDF: all report pdf/260909_daiwa_EPM.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-09
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

What’s new: Eternal Precision Mechanics (EPM) reported its August 2026 
revenue after market hours on 9 September 2026.

What’s the impact: August revenue beat expectations. EPM reported 
August 2026 revenue of TWD359.8m, up 95.7% MoM and 111.5% YoY. 
QTD revenue reached 88.4% of our previous 3Q26 forecast and 74.2% of 
the Bloomberg consensus. Given the faster-than-expected revenue 
recognition schedule and a sharp increase in recognised high-end 
equipment units, which should drive a more favourable product mix, we 
raise our 3Q26 revenue forecast by 33% to TWD972.7m, gross margin by 
3.1ppts to 48.5%, and EPS by 92% to TWD1.86.

Vacuum laminators are emerging as the next ABF bottleneck. Despite 
a partial conversion of backlog into revenue in August, EPM’s backlog 
continued to increase from TWD810m at end-July to TWD880m in early 
September. With lead times extending further to 36 months, vacuum 
laminators are emerging as the next bottleneck in the ABF capacity 
expansion cycle. Given the robust demand from ABF substrate makers, we 
see upside potential to EPM’s capacity expansion plans. On margins, we 
expect continued product-mix improvement and the full realisation of price 
increases to lift gross margin by 6.6ppts/1.3ppts YoY to 53.2%/54.5% in 
2027/28E, driving a 2026-28E EPS CAGR of 141%.

Moulding film adoption opens a new growth avenue. Traditional 
transfer and compression moulding typically use granular, powder, or liquid-
type compounds for encapsulation. During our visit to SEMICON, Japanese 
material suppliers Nagase (8012 JP, JPY1,150.5, Outperform [2]; covered 
by Masayuki Nagano) and Resonac (4004 JP, JPY16,070, Outperform [2]; 
covered by Hidemitsu Umebayashi) noted growing customer interest in 
moulding films. These offer better filling performance, better warpage 
control and higher flowability. This makes them increasingly suitable for 
advanced packaging applications such as panel level packaging and 
component-embedded substrates. EPM supplies moulding-film laminators 
for both applications, with revenue contribution expected to begin in 2027.

What we recommend: We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 22-26% to reflect the 
full realisation of price increases and improving product mix. We reaffirm 
our Buy (1) rating and raise our 12-month TP to TWD720 (from TWD600) 
based on an unchanged target PER of 40x, applied to our 1-year forward 
EPS forecast. Key downside risk: slower-than-expected substrate capex 
growth.

How we differ: We likely conduct a more comprehensive analysis than 
others on ABF industry demand and moulding film development.

9 September 2026

➢ Product mix and price hikes drive margins to new highs

Company profile 
Eternal was established in 2022 through the spin-off of Eternal Material’s equipment division and its 
subsequent merger with Nikko-Materials, a wholly owned Japanese subsidiary. The company was listed 
in January 2026 and is the global leader in IC substrate vacuum laminators, serving all major substrate 
manufacturers worldwide.

ESG analysis

Update Date: 26 Jun 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Eternal demonstrates a solid corporate governance structure supported by a balanced and
diversified board composition. The company’sboard consists of 7 members, including 3
independent directors, with backgrounds spanning industry operations, finance, accounting,
legal, and academia, enabling comprehensive oversight across business and governance
functions.
Capital management 2
Eternal adopts a prudent capital management strategy, supported by a stable shareholder
structure and disciplined financial policy. The company is majority-owned by Eternal Materials
(over 60% stake), providing long-term strategic backing and financial stability.
Related party &
transaction 1
Eternal discloses related-party transactions transparently and enforces strict governance
procedures to manage potential conflicts of interest. Board members are required to recuse
themselves from discussions and voting on matters involving personal or affiliated interests,
such as asset acquisitions, patent licensing, and remuneration decisions.
S Product quality &
safety 1
Eternal maintains strict product quality control, covering design, manufacturing, and delivery
processes. The company emphasizes equipment reliability and performance stability, supported
by long-term client relationships and low defect risks, ensuring high customer satisfaction and
consistent product safety standards.
E Product design &
lifecycle management 2
Eternal focuses on enhancing product durability and energy efficiency through continuous R&D
innovation. Its equipment design emphasizes precision, extended lifecycle and reduced material
waste, aligning product development with resource efficiency and long-term environmental
sustainability goals.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

Wistron

Reiterate Buy: Aug sales +49% MoM, aided 
by solid AI momentum; lift PO to NT$265

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 265.00 TWD | Price: 189.00 TWD

Aug’26 sales +49% MoM, mainly driven by AI 
Wistron’s Aug’26 sales of NT$460bn grew 49% MoM (+166% YoY), with subsidiary 
Wiwynn’s Aug sales of NT$144bn grew 23% MoM (+50% YoY). Wistron’s strong Aug 
sales were mainly driven by enterprise AI server projects, with overall AI server rack 
shipments roughly doubling MoM. Given the strong Aug sales, we lift our 3Q26 sales 
forecast by 11%. We expect Sep sales to decline 20% MoM to NT$370bn from the high 
Aug base. Yet, Sep sales should remain above July’s NT$308bn as we stay positive on AI 
order momentum into following quarters, driven by Nvidia’s new Vera Rubin generation.  
PC remains soft; Aug shipments down 13% MoM 
Wistron’s Aug NB shipments were 1.4mn (-13% MoM, -36% YoY). We view it as in line 
with supply chain’s commentary on weaker demand in 2H26 amid continued component 
cost inflation issue. This also echoes Wistron management’s cautious tone that PC 
shipment should decline 10-20% HoH in 2H26 following earlier order pull-in 1H26. We 
now expect 3Q26 NB shipment to fall 18% QoQ, implying 1.5mn units of NB shipments 
in Sep (+8% MoM, -31% YoY) with potential quarter-end pull-in demand. 
Lift PO to NT$265; reiterate Buy on solid AI momentum 
We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 4-6% to reflect higher sales, mainly driven by AI (with 
diversifying clients/projects), better operating leverage, and actual 2Q26 data. We raise 
our PO to NT$265 from NT$238, with our valuation method unchanged at 15x 2H26-
1H27E EPS. We reiterate Buy on Wistron’s solid multi-year AI server outlook. We expect 
a positive near-term market reaction to the strong Aug sales.

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
Wistron is one of the top-5 ODMs globally. Server/storage 
and PC business accounted for 73% and 24% of sales in 
2025, respectively. Key server/storage customers include 
Dell, Lenovo, HPE, Cisco, Nvidia, AMD, Intel. Key NB 
customers include Dell, Lenovo, Acer. Wistron was spun off 
from Acer (2353 TT) in 2000 and was incorporated in May 
2001. It went publicly listed on the Taiwan stock exchange 
in Aug 2003. It is headquartered in Taipei, Taiwan.

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to Wistron in view of improving 
sales/profit mix, solid outlook from Wiwynn, high exposure 
to server business after exiting from low-margin iPhone 
assembly business, and improving OPM. We believe 
Wistron will benefit from Dell's strong demand in Nvidia AI 
GPU server rack as the key supplier.

### Page 7

Price objective basis & risk 
Wistron (WICOF) 
Our PO of NT$265 is based on 15x 2H26-1H27E EPS. We use 15x valuation multiple 
(around 1.5SD above historical P/E average since 2022) in view of high server mix, a key 
position in AI server supply chain with exposure both to component/rack business, and 
ongoing OPM improvement.

Upside risks to our PO are (1) stronger server market demand, especially for AI, (2) 
stronger PC demand, (3) share/order win in AI server business, and (4) favorable FX 
trend.

Downside risks to our PO are (1) softer AI server demand due to AI GPU short supply or 
supply chain hiccup, (2) weaker PC market demand amid inflation/macro uncertainties, 
(3) GM pressure due to higher labor/logistics cost, (4) more intensified competition in 
NB and server business, and (5) unfavorable FX movement (USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) 
to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst ’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

Greater China Technology Hardware

Industry View In-Line
Our CY26 full-year forecast is now at 80-85K racks: This year is a strong one for
downstream rack assembly. We expect over 100% y/y growth in rack shipments, vs.

~26.5k last year.
Quanta (2382.TW, OW): August revenue was NT$424.0B (+16% m/m, +177% y/y).
We attribute the m/m growth mainly to higher GB200/300 rack shipments at 2.5-
2.55K racks (+22% m/m), despite offset slightly by lower NB shipments at 2.6M
units (-7% m/m, -37% y/y). Combining July and August GB200/300 rack shipments,
Quanta has already shipped ~4.6K racks, which would imply upside to our current
3Q forecast of ~6.4K racks. We think there is likely also upside to consensus

forecast, since combined July+August revenue reached ~NT$790B, which is 81% of
MSe and 71% of consensus 3Q revenue estimates. This also implies that there could
be upside to our full-year estimate of ~18.7K racks.
Wistron (3231.TW, OW): August revenue was  NT$460.1B (+49% m/m, +166% y/y).
We attribute the m/m growth to: 1) materially higher GB200/300 server computing
tray shipments  (2.3-2.4K rack equivalents, by our estimates, up approximately 100%

m/m), and higher Wiwynn revenue at ~NT$144.3B (+23% m/m, +50% y/y), while
offset by lower NB shipments at 1.4M units (-13% m/m, -36% y/y), lower DT
shipments at 500K (-29% m/m, -38% y/y) and lower monitor shipments at 750K (-
17% m/m, -6% y/y). Combined July + August revenue reached ~NT$768.3B, which is
74% of MSe and 75% of consensus 3Q revenue estimate, implying upside to 3Q. This
also implies that there could be upside to our full-year forecast of ~17.9K racks.

Hon Hai (2317.TW, OW): August revenue was NT$921.8B (-3% m/m, +52% y/y), with
server-related revenue seeing the strongest m/m growth, followed by components
and other products, while computing products and consumer segment, which mainly
includes iPhone assembly, declined m/m. We model HH's GB rack shipments at 2.6K
racks (+6% m/m). Our full-year forecast is unchanged, at ~31.7K racks.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We estimate GB200/300 rack output in August at ~8.4K (+27% m/m), within
which:
Quanta shipped 2.5-2.55K  GB200/GB300 racks.
Wistron shipped 2.3-2.4K GB200/GB300 racks.
Hon Hai shipped ~2.6K GB200/300 racks.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
Base case scenario value, derived from a residual income valuation model. Our key assump-
tions include a cost of equity of 8.5%, a medium-term growth rate of 13%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected iPhone sell-through
n Faster progress in AI server business
n Faster EV business development progress
n Any new M&A activity that could improve sentiment

Risks to Downside
n Lower iPhone sell-through
n Smaller profit contribution from AI business
n Slower EV business development progress
n Geopolitical developments that could negatively affect foreign investment
Quanta Computer Inc. (2382.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.0% (beta of
1.2, equity premium of 6.0% and risk-free rate of 1.5%), an 8.5% medium-term growth rate, and
a 3% terminal growth rate.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected NB demand
n Stronger-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Stronger-than-expected server demand
n Faster-than-expected AI server penetration
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected NB demand
n Softer-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Weak margin performance owing to rising labor costs and sales shortfalls
n Fierce price competition in the mega data center segment
n Slower-than-expected AI server penetration
